Boy Name Candidates - Vedic & Theravada
p. 1
 Boy Name Candidates
B / S / V  ·  1-3 syllables  ·  filtered & flagged
Lemma-based extraction from the Digital Corpus of Sanskrit (Vedic + Classical) and the Pali suttas
(Theravada, via the Digital Pali Dictionary). Conservatively filtered for a unique name: removed only clear girl
names (181), clearly-negative meanings (15), and pure grammatical/technical terms (28). Rivers, plant words,
feminine -a/-i endings, and terse attested male names are all kept on purpose.
6,715 candidates  |  1,708 attested names  |  590 with an evocative meaning.
Layout. Within each letter and syllable count, a Shortlist (attested names & evocative meanings) comes first,
then More below it.
Badges.  N = attested as a deity/person/epithet name  ·  + = evocative meaning  ·  f? = grammatically feminine
ending (say it aloud to judge)  ·  ! = harder consonant cluster.
Src. V = Vedic, C = Classical, T = Theravada. Full data incl. removed/no-gloss entries: upgraded_names.csv.
